//巴rvS14/28/VT4Bb_tomoe2147.「ちょっと、この後寄り道してもいいかしらっ？kvS14/28/VT4Bb_tomoe2148.　あなたの力が必要なの…お願い！」
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLine("<color=#9d7f69>巴</color>", NULL, "<color=#9d7f69>巴</color>", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	ModPlayVoiceLS(4, 28, "ps3/s14/28/vt4bb_tomoe2147", 256, TRUE);
	OutputLine(NULL, "「ちょっと、この後寄り道してもいいかしらっ？",
			NULL, "「接下来，我能稍微绕个路吗？", Line_WaitForInput);
	ModPlayVoiceLS(4, 28, "ps3/s14/28/vt4bb_tomoe2148", 256, TRUE);
	OutputLine(NULL, "　あなたの力が必要なの…お願い！」",
			NULL, "我需要你的帮助……拜托了！」", Line_Normal);
	ClearMessage();

	DisableWindow();
	SetValidityOfInput( FALSE );
	Wait( 3000 );
	DrawBustshotWithFiltering(6, "cinema", "x", 1, 0, 0, FALSE, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 25, 1300, TRUE );
	DrawBustshot(7, "title02", 0, 0, 0, FALSE, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 26, 3000, TRUE );
	Wait( 2000 );
	DrawBustshot(6, "black", 0, 0, 0, FALSE, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 25, 3000, TRUE );
	Wait( 1000 );
	FadeBustshotWithFiltering( 7, "x", 1, FALSE, 0, 0, 1000, TRUE );
	DrawScene( "black", 3000 );
	SetValidityOfInput( TRUE );

}
